Update JADE success-history memory\n   177\t             if len(SF) > 0:\n   178\t                 deltas = np.array(S_delta, dtype=float)\n   179\tdiff --git a/metabbo/param_controller.py b/metabbo/param_controller.py\n   180\tindex 1944065..476e195 100644\n   181\t--- a/metabbo/param_controller.py\n   182\t+++ b/metabbo/param_controller.py\n   183\t@@ -146,4 +146,56 @@ class AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n   184\t             epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\n   185\t \n   186\t         return {\'F\': float(F), \'CR\': float(CR), \'strategy\': strategy,\n   187\t-                \'epsilon\': float(epsilon)}\n   188\t\\ No newline at end of file\n   189\t+                \'epsilon\': float(epsilon)}\n   190\t+\n   191\t+\n   192\t+class LSHADEHeuristicController(ParamController):\n   193\t+    """Heuristic L-SHADE style controller for DE/JADE optimization.\n   194\t+\n   195\t+    Provides smooth progress-based adaptation of F, CR, and p_best,\n   196\t+    plus stagnation-triggered diversity boost. Designed for use with\n   197\t+    the L-SHADE style linear population size reduction in ec_algorithm.\n   198\t+    """\n   199\t+    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n   200\t+                 p_best_start=0.3, p_best_end=0.1, stagnation_boost=0.25,\n   201\t+                 stagnation_threshold=6):\n   202\t+        self.f_start = f_start\n   203\t+        self.f_end = f_end\n   204\t+        self.cr_start = cr_start\n   205\t+        self.cr_end = cr_end\n   206\t+        self.p_best_start = p_best_start\n   207\t+        self.p_best_end = p_best_end\n   208\t+        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n   209\t+        self.stagnation_threshold = stagnation_threshold\n   210\t+\n   211\t+    def reset(self, problem_meta):\n   212\t+        self.at = 0\n   213\t+        self.last_gbest = None\n   214\t+        self.stagnation_count = 0\n   215\t+\n   216\t+    def step(self, observation):\n   217\t+        self.at = observation.get(\'iteration\', self.at + 1)\n   218\t+        max_evals = float(observation.get(\'max_evals\', 10000))\n   219\t+        evals = float(observation.get(\'evals\', 0))\n   220\t+        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n   221\t+\n   222\t+        F = self.f_start * (1.0 - progress) + self.f_end * progress\n   223\t+        CR = self.cr_start * (1.0 - progress) + self.cr_end * progress\n   224\t+        p_best = self.p_best_start * (1.0 - progress) + self.p_best_end * progress\n   225\t+\n   226\t+        gbest = observation.get(\'gbest_f\', None)\n   227\t+        if gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n   228\t+            if gbest < self.last_gbest - Use archive for r2 50% of the time (vectorized)\\nx2 = pop[r2]\\nuse_arch = (archive.shape[0] > 0) & (rng.random(n) < 0.5)\\nif np.any(use_arch):\\n    x2[use_arch] = archive[rng.randint(0, archive.shape[0], size=int(np.sum(use_arch)))]\\n\\n# 5. Evaluate and select (ONLY acceptable per-individual loop, REQUIRED)\\nSF, SCR, S_delta = [], [], []\\nfor i in range(n):\\n    if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n        done = True\\n        break\\n    trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trials[i])\\n    evals += 1\\n    if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n        if archive.shape[0] < archive_size:\\n            archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1, -1)])\\n        else:\\n            archive[int(rng.randint(0, archive_size))] = pop[i]\\n        SF.append(F_i[i]); SCR.append(CR_i[i]); S_delta.append(fitness[i] - trial_f)\\n        pop[i] = trials[i]; fitness[i] = trial_f\\n        if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n            gbest_f = trial_f; gbest = trials[i].copy()\\n\\n# 8.
